- **polypilot-skeptic**: Red-teams a PROPOSED Polymarket trade — actively tries to refute the thesis with disconfirming data and adverse order-book flow. Dispatch before placing a sizeable trade. Returns {survives, refutation, suggested_fair_prob, confidence}. It has no execution tools and never trades. ([xnetcat/polypilot](https://github.com/xnetcat/polypilot/blob/HEAD/agents/polypilot-skeptic.md))
- **polyrepo-shipper**: Routes commit/push across Gestalt's registered git repos (hub + product remotes). Detects which clones have changes, asks the owner when the target is ambiguous, commits and pushes only dirty repos (in parallel when safe), so each remote's deploy can fire. Use when the owner says commit/push/ship code and more than one .git may be involved. Not for quest-log closeout alone (/ship-quest), not for inventing commit messages with no diff, not for force-push or rewriting history unless the owner explicitly orders it. ([aland3r/gestalt-kit](https://github.com/aland3r/gestalt-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/polyrepo-shipper.md))
- **polytropos-implementer**: Implements exactly one polytropos kit task brief, as written, and stops rather than improvising when the brief conflicts with the repository. Use when a kit task's brief is handed to you by the polytropos-execute skill or the cursor_execute driver. ([agentmc15/polytropos](https://github.com/agentmc15/polytropos/blob/HEAD/cursor/agents/polytropos-implementer.md))
- **polytropos-verifier**: Fresh-context, read-only verification of one completed polytropos kit task against its acceptance criteria; reruns the verify command and never trusts the implementer's claims. Use when the polytropos-execute skill or a reviewer asks for an independent check of a task or a phase. ([agentmc15/polytropos](https://github.com/agentmc15/polytropos/blob/HEAD/cursor/agents/polytropos-verifier.md))
- **polyvers**: Autonomously check whether a state-machine version change is safe against the live fleet — classify it into compatibility lanes, run the mechanical gates (round-trip, stimuli replay, migration validation, seeded model check) over fleet snapshots, scaffold the migration when the shape changed, and return the compat-report with failures triaged. Use to version-gate a machine change without step-by-step supervision. No API key. ([cognitive-fab/polygraph](https://github.com/cognitive-fab/polygraph/blob/HEAD/agents/polyvers.md) · ★16)
- **polyviz**: Autonomously render Polygraph verification artifacts into a clean, brand-consistent diagram set (SVG, optional PNG) and return where the figures landed. Given a viz-model JSON or a Polygraph/polyvers artifacts directory, produce the catalog (state-machine, invariants, counterexample, compat-gate, model-card), deterministically, with no model call at render time. Use to generate the figures for a report/post/deck without step-by-step supervision. Does not run verification — it only visualizes artifacts other engines produced. ([cognitive-fab/polygraph](https://github.com/cognitive-fab/polygraph/blob/HEAD/polyviz/agents/polyviz.md) · ★16)
- **pom-council-synthesizer**: Synthesizes per-role outputs from a pom-council run into a tension narrative. Reads {disc_context, role_outputs[]} and returns {tensions[], synthesis_prose}. Amplifies disagreements, never softens them. Never produces a verdict — verdict tokens are forbidden in output. Invoked only by the pom-council skill. ([swanson-dev/product-operating-model](https://github.com/swanson-dev/product-operating-model/blob/HEAD/plugins/pom-core/agents/pom-council-synthesizer.md) · ★1)
- **pom-discovery-auditor**: Independently grades a Discovery item against the four-question gate, without seeing the original grading. Provides a second-opinion verdict (✅/🟡/❌ per question) with rationale. Read-only. Use when a Discovery decision is high-stakes, controversial, or near the promote threshold. ([swanson-dev/product-operating-model](https://github.com/swanson-dev/product-operating-model/blob/HEAD/plugins/pom-core/agents/pom-discovery-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **pom-ethics-reviewer**: Independently reviews a Discovery item's Q4 (ethical / responsible) grading, pushing back on weakly evidenced ✅ marks. Industry-aware — uses the generic Q4 framing plus any installed industry overlay framing. Read-only. Use after /pom-discovery-gate for high-stakes items, or in /pom-review for batch re-audits. ([swanson-dev/product-operating-model](https://github.com/swanson-dev/product-operating-model/blob/HEAD/plugins/pom-core/agents/pom-ethics-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **pom-portfolio-auditor**: Scans the entire POM portfolio and surfaces structural and flow-level issues: stuck UCs, stale runways, orphan ADRs, products without pods, broken disposition→Discovery→Backlog chains. Returns findings grouped by category. Read-only. Used by /pom-review to produce stakeholder-ready summaries. ([swanson-dev/product-operating-model](https://github.com/swanson-dev/product-operating-model/blob/HEAD/plugins/pom-core/agents/pom-portfolio-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **pom-trio**: Provides a single role's framing on a Discovery item or product decision — Product Manager (viability), Product Designer (desirability), Technical Leader (feasibility), Scrum Master (process), or Product Owner (acceptance signals). Invoke with role=pm|pd|tl|sm|po as the first prompt argument. Reads the target DISC/PB/product files, never writes. Use when a Discovery item needs a Trio-style review without convening four humans. ([swanson-dev/product-operating-model](https://github.com/swanson-dev/product-operating-model/blob/HEAD/plugins/pom-core/agents/pom-trio.md) · ★1)
- **pom-uc-researcher**: Researches a raw stakeholder source (PDF, deck, markdown, transcript) and produces a structured pre-UC dossier — problem statement, stakeholders, outcomes, evidence, what's already true, open questions — ready for WSJF scoring by pom-wsjf-scorer or pom-ingest-use-case. Read-only on the POM repo. Use proactively when ingesting external materials before scoring. ([swanson-dev/product-operating-model](https://github.com/swanson-dev/product-operating-model/blob/HEAD/plugins/pom-core/agents/pom-uc-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **pom-validator**: Independently scans a POM repo against the full validation ruleset and returns structured ERROR/WARN/INFO findings as JSON. Used by /pom-review and /pom-validate when the parent context needs validation findings without consuming context window with raw file reads. Read-only. ([swanson-dev/product-operating-model](https://github.com/swanson-dev/product-operating-model/blob/HEAD/plugins/pom-core/agents/pom-validator.md) · ★1)
- **pom-wsjf-scorer**: Given a pre-UC dossier (from pom-uc-researcher) plus the active WSJF rubric, proposes WSJF scores per dimension with cited evidence and explicit anchor references. Does NOT write files — proposes scores for human confirmation. Use when pom-ingest-use-case wants an evidence-grounded first-cut score before prompting the user. ([swanson-dev/product-operating-model](https://github.com/swanson-dev/product-operating-model/blob/HEAD/plugins/pom-core/agents/pom-wsjf-scorer.md) · ★1)

- **ponchi-drawer**: バイブコーディング図鑑の1エントリぶん擬人化ポンチ絵SVGを書き起こす専門エージェント。黒い手書き風の線画で、誌面ポンチ絵メモに沿った 1 コマを作る。draft用途で、後日画像生成に差し替える前提。 ([Taguchi-1989/VibeCodingDictionary](https://github.com/Taguchi-1989/VibeCodingDictionary/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ponchi-drawer.md) · ★1)
- **ponder-reviewer**: Use when a pull request, module, security boundary, or product flow needs a coverage-gated, multi-pass deliberative review where every unit is graded and false positives are retracted before reporting. - **pong**: Pong Agent ([42tg/claude-ping-pong](https://github.com/42tg/claude-ping-pong/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pong.md))
- **ponytail**: The lazy senior dev. Adversarial reviewer that attacks a changeset for bloat, unnecessary abstraction, and rewrites, and forces the laziest Solution that actually works — YAGNI, reuse before rewrite, stdlib and native before dependencies, one line before fifty. Use to review a diff for size. Also dispatchable for an implementation the architect names explicitly. Reads Claude.md files for stack-specific patterns. ([heyJordanParker/dotfiles](https://github.com/heyJordanParker/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/packages/agents/agents/ponytail.md) · ★22)
- **ponytail**: Builder and owner. Owns one slice or one PR end to end: build, test, prove it works on the real artifact, commit, and, when it runs as an Autopilot owner, merge. May spawn its own subagents. Spawn a fresh ponytail for each new task, fix round, or follow-up, and resume one only in the strict cases that the lead skill's Subagents section names under Fresh subagents by default. Reads the lead skill's SKILL.md in full before any work. ([alexnthnz/keel](https://github.com/alexnthnz/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/ponytail.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **ponytail**: Read-only lazy senior developer that finds the simplest, shortest, most minimal solution that actually works. Use when you want the smallest root-cause fix, a YAGNI check on a plan, or a second opinion on whether new code needs to exist at all. Pass the level (lite, full, ultra) in the prompt; default is full. ([segersniels/dotfiles](https://github.com/segersniels/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ponytail.md))
- **ponytail**: Lazy senior developer mode. Solves problems with the least new code, stdlib first, YAGNI, and rigorous verification. ([SLedgehammer-dev12/Heat_Exchanger_Calc](https://github.com/SLedgehammer-dev12/Heat_Exchanger_Calc/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/ponytail.md))
- **ponytail**: Lazy senior developer mode. Solves problems with the smallest complete change, stdlib first, YAGNI, and rigorous verification. ([SLedgehammer-dev12/Heat_Exchanger_Calc](https://github.com/SLedgehammer-dev12/Heat_Exchanger_Calc/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/ponytail.md))
- **ponytail**: For mechanical, low-blast-radius work where the orchestrator (or a worker agent) already made every decision. The micro-model agent executes; it does not judge. - **ponytail-reviewer**: Read-only pragmatic-skeptic reviewer — the "lazy senior dev" who has been at the company longer than version control. Interrogates a change for over-engineering and over-strictness: code, abstractions, dependencies, files, or process steps that the problem does not actually need. The deliberate complement to `devils-advocate` — that agent fights under-validation (confirmation bias, "should we build this?"); this one fights over-building ("is this more code/process than the problem needs?"). Returns a 6-section structured critique. Read-only and advisory: it flags reductions, it never applies… ([wildcard/caro](https://github.com/wildcard/caro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ponytail-reviewer.md) · ★41)
- **ponytail-reviewer**: Over-engineering PR review specialist.
